# Desktop app feature parity: TurboSpark Swift app vs ZCode and Unsloth Desktop

Analysis date: 2026-09-26. This document tracks feature parity between this repository's macOS
desktop app `swift/TurboSparkApp` (native SwiftUI app for local LLM inference over the Rust/Metal
runtime, with a Claude Code-style coding agent) and two sibling desktop products:

- Part A: Zcode (`../Zcode`, ZCode v3.14.3 AI coding workbench: Electron desktop app + web client +
  HTTP/WS server + agent CLI/TUI/runtime, TypeScript).
- Part B: Unsloth Desktop (`../unsloth`, the unslothai/unsloth checkout whose `studio/` directory is
  Unsloth Studio / Unsloth Desktop: a Tauri shell around a Python FastAPI backend and a React
  frontend, driving the unsloth training and inference stack).

Method (both parts): every folder and file group of the sibling checkout was assigned to an analysis
pass. For each feature the table links the original source and the TurboSpark Swift source when one
exists. Status is `Full` (equivalent capability exists in the Swift app), `Partial` (exists but
narrower; the gap and a concrete improvement are noted), or `Missing` (no equivalent; the note
describes what implementing it would involve).

## Part A: ZCode vs TurboSpark Swift app

### Part A method and results

Method: every folder and file group of the Zcode checkout was assigned to an analysis pass covering
all 15 packages, the agent workspace `apps/zcode-cli`, and the repo-level config, harness,
third-party, public, scripts, and doc material.

Results across 530 compared features: 132 Full, 169 Partial, 229 Missing. TurboSpark is a
single-machine local-inference app with no cloud backend, so a large share of the Missing rows are
cloud-service features by design (accounts/OAuth login, billing and subscription quotas, telemetry
upload, remote SSH/WSL/Docker workspaces, hosted marketplaces and share pages, web client, IM bot
channels). The recurring genuine capability gaps, in rough priority order:

1. Dynamic workflow engine (scripted multi-subagent orchestration with journals, resume, amendment):
   Zcode has a full compiler plus runtime; TurboSpark declares a Workflow tool but never implements it.
2. Browser automation and computer use (embedded browser, CDP control, screen/keyboard agent
   operations with permission onboarding): absent everywhere in TurboSpark.
3. Node REPL execution (declared-only tool in TurboSpark; Zcode ships a sandboxed persistent-VM host).
4. Git write operations (commit, push, staging, AI commit messages, workspace checkpoints/restore):
   TurboSpark's git layer is read-only plus checkout/init.
5. Chat-quality-of-life gaps: session title auto-generation, microcompact, streaming recovery,
   cross-session references (#sess_), queued-input editing, file-turn snapshots, user-defined
   markdown slash commands, external-config import beyond MCP, heatmap/chart usage views.
6. Command palette (Cmd+K over commands/files, not just chats) and composer drag-and-drop, image
   paste from NSPasteboard, and mermaid rendering.
7. Process lifecycle hardening: supervised daemon with crash budget, launchd registration, log
   export/retention, unified logging, per-process resource sampling.

Where TurboSpark leads: 21 locales vs 2, encrypted SQLCipher profile vault with biometric unlock,
embedded OpenAI/Anthropic/Ollama-compatible local server, hardware-fit model install/management,
steering vectors, goals with idle check-ins, hooks with 13 lifecycle events vs 7, native search
without external binaries, and TTS read-aloud.

## 1. Agent runtime core, packages/core (part 1)

Scope: `apps/zcode-cli/packages/core` runtime, agent, repl, session-context, runtime-task, compact,
context, permission, memory, system-reminder, errors.

A small sidecar completion over the first user message plus a title-updated event would cover it. |
| [Hooks runtime integration](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/runtime/methods/hooks.ts): hook invocation at lifecycle events, hook context as system reminders, session-start once-per-session guard | [hooks.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/runtime/methods/hooks.ts), [internal-hook-methods.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/runtime/internal-hook-methods.ts) | [AppHookExecutionEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookExecutionEngine.swift) | Full | Equivalent lifecycle hook engines; the core-side wiring mirrors TurboSpark's 13-event hook system. |
| [Internal plumbing](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/runtime/methods/residency.ts): residency tracking for detached runtime work, runtime info, ANSI/JSON output formatting | [residency.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/runtime/methods/residency.ts), [environment.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/environment.ts) | [AppModel.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel.swift) | Partial | Session-pool residency has no TurboSpark counterpart (single-app model keeps chats in memory); runtime info parallels ProcessInfo and output formatting parallels the ANSI colorizer. Mostly internal plumbing. |

## 2. Agent runtime core, packages/core (part 2)

Scope: mcp, hooks, workflow, browser-client, embedded-search, model, telemetry, plugin-reference.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [MCP tool bridge](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts): projects MCP server tool descriptors into the registry as model-callable tools with mcp__server__tool namespacing, allow/disallow filtering, name aliases | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts), [name.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/name.ts) | [McpTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpTools.swift), [McpClientEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpClientEngine.swift) | Full | Both register external MCP tools for the model. Zcode adds per-tool result budgets, default 30s timeout with override, cancellation contract, and trace propagation per entry; TurboSpark handles these more coarsely. |
| [MCP annotation-driven risk](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts): maps readOnlyHint/destructiveHint to risk level, approval requirement, concurrency safety, side-effect scope | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts) | [McpClientEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpClientEngine.swift), [McpServerSpec.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpServerSpec.swift) | Full | Both parse annotations and fold them into risk as hints; Zcode additionally stamps denyPriority and network/system sideEffectScope used by the permission engine. |
| [MCP result normalization](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/image-normalization.ts): converts text/image/audio/resource blocks to model content, caps inlined base64 images, routes oversized output to session artifacts | [image-normalization.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/image-normalization.ts) | none | Partial | TurboSpark MCP result handling has no image-block or byte-budget logic, so image-returning servers would degrade. Can be improved by detecting image blocks in McpTools and rendering them in ToolCallCardView, storing oversized payloads as artifacts. |
| [Official computer-use (CUA) trust gate](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts): only authority-verified servers may project canonical mcp__computer-use__ names, dedicated capability group, protected frame content; fail-closed | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/mcp/index.ts) | none | Missing | No screen-observation/computer-use capability in TurboSpark. Would need a verified server identity channel, a screen-capture tool over a trusted bridge, and pixel-coordinate content protection. |
| [Lifecycle hook engine](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/runner.ts): matcher engine, ordered execution, per-hook timeouts, abort linkage, admission re-check before each dispatch, lifecycle events, decision semantics (block, additionalContext, permission decisions, stop continuation) | [runner.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/runner.ts), [output.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/output.ts) | [AppHookExecutionEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookExecutionEngine.swift), [AppHookMatcher.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookMatcher.swift) | Full | Equivalent Claude Code-style hook contract on both sides. Nuance: Zcode contracts 7 lifecycle events while TurboSpark supports 13 (adds SessionEnd, SubagentStart/Stop, Notification, PreCompact, PermissionDenied); Zcode re-verifies trust admission per dispatch. |
| [Configured hook registration](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/configured-runner.ts): builds registrations from settings/plugin config, command-type execution, timeout resolution, output byte caps, plugin variable expansion, display-text sanitization | [configured-runner.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/configured-runner.ts), [configured-runner-callback.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/configured-runner-callback.ts) | [AppHookStore+Discovery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookStore+Discovery.swift), [AppHookCommandRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookCommandRunner.swift) | Partial | Zcode implements only the "command" hook type; TurboSpark defines command, http, and prompt hook types. Zcode could add HTTP and prompt (model-judged) hook backends; TurboSpark already covers the gap. |
| [Workspace hook trust and review flow](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/workspace-hook-trust-coordinator.ts): digest-based hook bundle admission, trust evaluation and revocation, user review flow with generations and supersede handling | [workspace-hook-trust-coordinator.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/workspace-hook-trust-coordinator.ts), [workspace-hook-review-flow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/workspace-hook-review-flow.ts) | [AppHookStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookStore.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark trusts individual hook command hashes with a source detail UI; Zcode trusts versioned workspace bundles with explicit review/revoke lifecycle and revalidation at each execution. Can be improved with bundle-level digests and a pending-review flow. |
| [Session mailbox drain hooks](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/session-mailbox.ts): drains unread session mailbox messages and enqueues them as pending input at PostToolUse (remote companion messaging into a live run) | [session-mailbox.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/hooks/session-mailbox.ts) | none | Missing | No remote inbox concept; TurboSpark queues prompts locally only. Maps naturally onto AppModel+Queue.swift with a SessionMailbox port. |

Needs a snapshot store, child-session phase orchestration, and artifact writing. |
| [Workflow graph scheduler](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/scheduler.ts): DAG node scheduling with ready-node ordering, blocked-node detection, LLM planner expansion of node collections, event log, snapshot persistence | [scheduler.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/scheduler.ts), [collection-planner.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/scheduler/collection-planner.ts) | none | Missing | No dynamic graph execution engine in TurboSpark; the BatchTool covers only flat parallelism, not planner-expanded DAGs. |
| [Workflow snapshot lifecycle](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/lifecycle.ts): resume reconciliation of stale runs, node reopen with caps, run cancellation, phase bookkeeping | [lifecycle.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/lifecycle.ts) | none | Missing | Would ride along with the workflow engine; TurboSpark task list tools have no resumable run snapshots. |
| [Workflow critic loop and parsers](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/expert/critic-loop.ts): final critic iterations with verdicts, critic/planner/graph-seed/node-prompt/JSON parsers, retry state, graph artifacts | [critic-loop.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/expert/critic-loop.ts), [formatters.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/workflow/expert/formatters.ts) | none | Missing | Related but different: TurboSpark's GoalEvaluator judges /goal progress in chat; no structured workflow critic or artifact parsers. |

Would need a host browser bridge (WKWebView or extension backend) and a command transport. |

## 3. Repo config, harness, third-party, root docs

Scope: `DESIGN.md`, `CONTEXT.md`, `NOTICE.md`, `README.md`/`README.en.md`, `architecture-policy.yaml`,
`config/`, `.env.*`, `mise.toml`, `package.json` workspace scripts, `.vscode/`, `harness/remote/`,
`patches/`, `third-party/`, `public/`.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Codified design system](../../Zcode/DESIGN.md): mandatory type scale tokens, semantic color roles, waiting-badge and workflow-timeline token families, agent-facing UI rules | [DESIGN.md](../../Zcode/DESIGN.md) | [ResolvedAppTheme.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/ResolvedAppTheme.swift), [AppAppearance.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/AppAppearance.swift) | Full | Both enforce a themed design vocabulary; Zcode ships it as a portable markdown contract for coding agents, TurboSpark as compiled theme code. TurboSpark could mirror the doc-as-contract idea for its own agent-driven UI work. |
| [Module boundary policy](../../Zcode/architecture-policy.yaml): machine-readable per-module ownership, dependency requirements, max file lines, forbidden cycles and deep imports | [architecture-policy.yaml](../../Zcode/architecture-policy.yaml) | none | Missing | TurboSpark governs layering via AGENTS.md prose and SwiftPM targets, with no automated boundary checker. A small script enforcing max file size and forbidden imports per crate/target would be the equivalent. |
| [Built-in client config with remote fallback](../../Zcode/config/default.json): feedback URL, per-language community links, fetched from a remote client-configs endpoint with 1h cache and built-in fallback | [default.json](../../Zcode/config/default.json), [config/README.md](../../Zcode/config/README.md) | none | Missing | TurboSpark has no feedback or community entry points. Adding a General-settings help card with feedback/community links would be low effort. |
| [Built-in provider config](../../Zcode/config/provider/zcode-builtin.json): default cloud provider configuration shipped with the client, overridable via env var | [zcode-builtin.json](../../Zcode/config/provider/zcode-builtin.json) | none | Missing | By design: TurboSpark runs local models through its catalog and does not ship cloud provider credentials or endpoints. |
| [Environment layering and toolchain pinning](../../Zcode/.env.example): .env/.env.local override layers, dev/prod service endpoints, mise-pinned Node/pnpm versions | [.env.example](../../Zcode/.env.example), [mise.toml](../../Zcode/mise.toml) | [docs/DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md), [Makefile](Makefile) | Partial | TurboSpark pins toolchains via repo docs and Makefile targets but has no layered env file mechanism; its few tunables (HF endpoint mirror, TURBOSPARK_HOME) are handled in code. Documenting a single .env-style override file would simplify machine-local setup. |
| [Product notice and risk disclosure](../../Zcode/NOTICE.md): feature scope, execution/data risk table, permission-mode semantics, licensing summary | [NOTICE.md](../../Zcode/NOTICE.md) | [NOTICE](NOTICE), [LICENSE](LICENSE) | Partial | TurboSpark ships LICENSE and NOTICE and documents guardrails in docs/, but has no user-facing risk/permission disclosure surfaced in the app; a first-run or About-panel summary would close the gap. |
| [Third-party notice pipeline](../../Zcode/third-party/inventory.json): license inventory, upstream license texts, copied/embedded component tracking, generated THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md wired into distributions | [inventory.json](../../Zcode/third-party/inventory.json), [generate-third-party-notices.mjs](../../Zcode/scripts/generate-third-party-notices.mjs) | [Resources/Licenses/](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Resources/Licenses/) | Partial | TurboSpark bundles per-dependency license files (SQLCipher, fonts, icons) manually; no generator producing a consolidated notice inside the app bundle. A build step assembling Resources/Licenses into one view would match it. |
| [Remote workspace harness](../../Zcode/harness/remote/Dockerfile): Docker-based remote dev/workspace harness for SSH/WSL/container sessions | [Dockerfile](../../Zcode/harness/remote/Dockerfile), [README.md](../../Zcode/harness/remote/README.md) | none | Missing | TurboSpark is single-machine by design; no remote workspace concept anywhere in the Swift app. |
| [Dependency patches](../../Zcode/patches/@ai-sdk__anthropic@3.0.81.patch): pinned patches to upstream npm packages (provider SDKs, telemetry SDK removal) | [@ai-sdk__anthropic@3.0.81.patch](../../Zcode/patches/@ai-sdk__anthropic@3.0.81.patch) | none | Partial | Same need handled differently: TurboSpark vendors code (SQLCipher checkout, Lucide icons with manifests) rather than patching upstream packages; no mechanism is needed while dependencies are few. |
| [Editor/dev launch configs](../../Zcode/.vscode/launch.json): committed VS Code launch/tasks for Electron and Vite dev loops | [launch.json](../../Zcode/.vscode/launch.json), [tasks.json](../../Zcode/.vscode/tasks.json) | none | Missing | TurboSpark development runs through the Makefile and Xcode; no committed editor session configs. |
| [Branding assets](../../Zcode/public/logo/icons/1024x1024.png): product logo set | [1024x1024.png](../../Zcode/public/logo/icons/1024x1024.png) | [Resources/AppIcon.icns](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Resources/AppIcon.icns) | Full | Both ship full brand asset sets; TurboSpark adds mascots, dock icon presets, and bundled fonts. |
| [Bilingual product README](../../Zcode/README.md): zh-CN and English user documentation for install, dev, and packaging | [README.md](../../Zcode/README.md), [README.en.md](../../Zcode/README.en.md) | [docs/README.md](docs/README.md) | Partial | TurboSpark documentation is English-only and developer-oriented; the Swift app itself ships 21 localizations, so user-facing bilingual docs would be consistent with the app's localization depth. |

## 4. Agent app wiring, packages/bootstrap

Scope: `apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap` (app assembly, wire protocols, plugin/workflow/cron
services). Concrete built-in tool executors live in other agent packages; this section is at the
adapter/config/port level. The zcode-protocol and zcode-protocol-v4 subtrees are client-server
plumbing with no TurboSpark equivalent because TurboSpark is a monolithic local app.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [App assembly](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/create-app.ts): composes Node adapters (fs, exec, http, image, pdf, mcp, skills, mailbox, artifact store) into AgentRuntime plus facades | [create-app.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/create-app.ts), [types.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/types.ts) | [AppModel.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel.swift), [AppModel+Tools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+Tools.swift) | Partial | AppModel performs equivalent monolithic assembly; no port/adapter abstraction. Can be improved by factoring tool execution behind injectable port interfaces. |
| [Layered runtime config](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/runtime-config.ts): user/project/env/cli layers, permission mode, MCP servers, hooks, untrusted project MCP gating | [runtime-config.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/runtime-config.ts) | [AppProject.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppProject.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark has per-project permission modes and MCP project config detection, but no explicit untrusted-project-server admission gate; flagging project-declared MCP servers as untrusted until approved would match it. |
| [Permission options projection](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/permission-options.ts): session always-allow option, structured deny content fed back to the model | [permission-options.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/permission-options.ts) | [AppToolPermissionEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Core/AppToolPermissionEngine.swift) | Full | TurboSpark permission engine has allow/ask/deny with a session always-allow store; Zcode adds provider-visible deny text, which TurboSpark could mirror for better model recovery. |
| [Protocol v3 agent server](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/server.ts): stdio NDJSON JSON-RPC with session/workspace/mcp/plugins/automation methods, session resident pool with idle TTL and LRU eviction | [server.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/server.ts), [session-resident-pool.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/session-resident-pool.ts) | none | Missing | TurboSpark binds SwiftUI directly to AppModel; no external process protocol. Implementing would mean exposing AppModel operations as a JSON-RPC surface for headless/remote clients. |
| [Protocol v4 gateway](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol-v4/v4-gateway.ts): event-sourced conversation delta log, product projection reducer, command inbox with admission guards, sessions-index and workspace-config topics | [v4-gateway.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol-v4/v4-gateway.ts), [product-projection.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol-v4/product-projection.ts) | none | Missing | The wire projection layer has no counterpart in the monolithic app; would involve publishing AppModel state changes as replayable ConversationDelta frames. |
| [Interaction broker](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/interaction-broker.ts): permission requests and AskUserQuestion as reverse requests to the client, first-answer-wins registry | [interaction-broker.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/interaction-broker.ts) | [InteractiveQuestionCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/InteractiveQuestionCardView.swift), [PlanningInteractiveTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Planning/PlanningInteractiveTools.swift) | Full | User-facing capability (model asks the user structured questions and permission decisions mid-turn) exists; the wire race logic is CLI-specific plumbing TurboSpark does not need. |
| [Skills wiring](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/skills.ts): discovery via skill adapter with plugin and bundled roots, per-skill disable overrides, bundled skill pack with content-hash extraction | [skills.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/skills.ts), [bundled-skills.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/bundled-skills.ts) | [SkillManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SkillManager.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark discovers user/project/bundled/plugin skills with a budgeted skill tool, but has no per-skill disable override config. Can be improved by adding a skillOverrides-style settings key. |
| [Slash command surface](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/custom-commands.ts): user/project custom commands with $ARGUMENTS and shell expansion, reserved names, builtin /init and /workflow prompt commands | [custom-commands.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/custom-commands.ts), [custom-command-prompt.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/custom-command-prompt.ts) | [BuiltInSlashCommand.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/BuiltInSlashCommand.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark has a rich builtin slash set and plugin-declared commands, but no user-defined custom command directory discovery or /init. Can be improved by scanning command folders with template/shell expansion. |
| [Subagent profiles](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/subagents.ts): markdown agent profile loading (user, plugin, built-in names, model selection overrides), live subagent observation for UI | [subagents.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/subagents.ts) | [AppAgentDefinition.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppAgentDefinition.swift), [SubagentRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SubagentRunner.swift) | Full | Both have markdown-defined agents, reserved builtin names, depth caps, and live subagent cards; Zcode adds plugin-contributed agents with model overrides, a small gap. |

## 5. Agent infrastructure adapters, packages/adapters

Scope: `apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters` - the CLI's external-I/O layer (model providers, auth,
MCP transports, browser, PDF/image, exec, network, storage, skills, plugins, config, logging).

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cloud LLM provider adapters (AI SDK: Anthropic, OpenAI, OpenAI-compatible endpoints, API-key auth, per-provider fetch pipelines) | [model-execution.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/model-execution.ts) | none | Missing | TurboSpark is local-FFI-only by design; its embedded server instead exposes OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible endpoints for external clients (Sources/TurboSparkApp/Server/). Consuming cloud APIs would require an API-key/account layer the app deliberately omits. |
| Model runner (streaming generate, retry budget/backoff, failure classification, request admission, idle timeouts) | [runner.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/runner.ts), [retry-policy.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/retry-policy.ts) | [AppModel+AgentLoop.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+AgentLoop.swift), [ForgeGuardrailsEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Guardrails/ForgeGuardrailsEngine.swift) | Partial | FFI streaming loop with cancellation and Forge retry nudges exists, but there is no generalized backoff/retry-budget or structured failure-reason taxonomy; adding retry policy and failure classification around the FFI session would close it. |
| Official Coding Plan gateway (route official model endpoints through the platform gateway, API-key resolution) | [official-coding-plan-gateway.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/official-coding-plan-gateway.ts) | none | Missing | Cloud subscription routing; would require a platform gateway and accounts, out of scope for a local-only app. |
| Model wire-compat shims (Anthropic thinking-stream rewrite, OpenAI Responses JSON normalization, strict tool schema folding) | [anthropic-stream-compat.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/anthropic-stream-compat.ts), [strict-tool-schema.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/strict-tool-schema.ts) | [ForgeGuardrailsEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Guardrails/ForgeGuardrailsEngine.swift) | Partial | Not needed for local FFI; TurboSpark's Forge rescuing malformed tool calls is the related capability. Folding unsupported JSON Schema constraints into descriptions when advertising tools to server clients would be the improvement. |
| Tool call stream assembly and validation (partial-arg assembler, name/input normalization, empty-completion retry, tool result media projection) | [streaming-tool-call-assembler.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/streaming-tool-call-assembler.ts), [tool-call-validation.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/tool-call-validation.ts) | [AppModel+AgentLoop.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+AgentLoop.swift) | Partial | Tool-call extraction and malformed-call rescue exist; no incremental JSON assembly of long tool args or media projection of tool results. |
| CLI OAuth login (Z.ai / BigModel authorize URL, flow polling, token exchange, browser handoff) | [cli-oauth.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/auth/cli-oauth.ts), [bigmodel-oauth.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/auth/bigmodel-oauth.ts) | none | Missing | Inherently cloud-account login; would need an OAuth client, browser handoff, and remote account service. |
| Credential storage (shared credential file, AES-256-GCM cipher, localhost OAuth callback server) | [shared-credentials.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/auth/shared-credentials.ts), [credential-cipher.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/auth/credential-cipher.ts) | [ProfileVault.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProfileVault.swift), [ProfileVaultCrypto.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProfileVaultCrypto.swift) | Partial | Local secrets are already protected by passphrase + biometric AES-GCM vault and Keychain; there is no OAuth token store or callback server since there is no login. Vault crypto could be reused if token storage is ever added. |
| MCP client adapter (JSON-RPC client, stdio/SSE/streamable-HTTP transports, ping liveness, reconnect, connection pool) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mcp/index.ts), [pool.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mcp/pool.ts) | [McpClientEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpClientEngine.swift), [McpServerSpec.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/MCP/McpServerSpec.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark's McpServerSpec has only stdio and sse cases: no streamable HTTP transport, no connection pool, no ping-based liveness detection. Adding an HTTP transport case and health pings is a concrete improvement. |
| MCP OAuth (authorization code + PKCE, token refresh single-flight, 403 scope step-up, interactive transactions) | [oauth.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mcp/oauth.ts), [oauth-interactive.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mcp/oauth-interactive.ts) | none | Missing | Would involve a localhost callback server, discovery/DCR client, and encrypted token store; depends on the absent OAuth layer. |
| Official MCP auth (trusted-origin registry, identity headers injected via JSON-RPC _meta) | [official-auth.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mcp/official-auth.ts) | none | Missing | Tied to platform identity and Coding Plan entitlements; cloud-only service. |
| Browser automation adapter (managed headless Chromium via Playwright CDP, tabs, dialogs, viewport, page commands, URL allowlist) | [session.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/browser/session.ts), [page-command.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/browser/page-command.ts) | none | Missing | No browser automation anywhere in the Swift app. Implementing would mean bundling headless Chromium or automating WKWebView with a command router. |
| PDF processing (pdfinfo page counts, pdftoppm rasterization to JPEG for vision input, structured error taxonomy) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/pdf/index.ts) | [DocumentTextExtractor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Presentation/DocumentTextExtractor.swift) | Partial | PDF text extraction, QuickLook previews, and FileRead PDF pages mode exist, but there is no page rasterization to images for model input. Rendering PDF pages via PDFKit into base64 image attachments would be a strong improvement for local vision models. |
| Image processing (Jimp resize/compress to model image budget, media type detection, WebP passthrough) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/image/index.ts), [jimp-compression.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/image/jimp-compression.ts) | [FileReadWriteTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/FileReadWriteTools.swift) | Partial | FileRead returns base64 images for multimodal local models, but there is no budget-aware downscaling or recompression. CGImage-based resize and JPEG quality search before embedding would improve token cost. |
| HTTP client adapter (proxy-agent, custom CA bundle, DNS-resolution public-egress SSRF guard, response body caps) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/http/index.ts), [public-egress-policy.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/http/public-egress-policy.ts) | [WebFetchExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Web/WebFetchExecutor.swift), [HttpRequestExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Web/HttpRequestExecutor.swift) | Full | Equivalent WebFetch + HttpRequest tools with SSRF private-host defense and domain allow/deny; nuance: no user-configurable proxy or custom CA override. |

| Filesystem adapter (text range reads with encoding/line-ending detection, binary reads, ripgrep-backed search, worker-thread decoding) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/fs/index.ts), [text-range-reader.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/fs/text-range-reader.ts) | [FileReadWriteTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/FileReadWriteTools.swift), [SyntextCodeSearchTool.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/SyntextCodeSearchTool.swift) | Full | Equivalent read/write/edit/glob/grep with Syntext ripgrep-format search plus FileSnapshotStore stale-write protection. |

Can be improved by scanning command dirs with the same root-priority logic as skills. |
| Plugins adapter (plugin.json manifests with commands/agents/skills/hooks/mcpServers contributions, marketplace sources: git, GitHub archive zip, arbitrary zip, official bundled+CDN partitions, atomic install) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/plugins/index.ts), [marketplace.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/plugins/marketplace.ts) | [PluginManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/PluginManager.swift), [PluginMarketplaceManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/PluginMarketplaceManager.swift) | Full | Claude Code-style plugin format with marketplace clone/pull, contributions namespacing, and install/update/uninstall all present; the official CDN marketplace partition is a cloud facet TurboSpark replaces with git/local marketplaces. |
| Config system (user config.json + project configs + env merge with priority, schema validation, change observers, feature flags) | [config-factory.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/config/config-factory.ts), [config-merger.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/config/config-merger.ts) | [MacAppSettings.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/MacAppSettings.swift) | Partial | Rich 16-tab settings UI with per-feature toggles exists, but there is no layered file/env config pipeline with schema validation or observers. JSON import/export of full settings profiles would be a practical improvement. |
| Context source adapter (env info: cwd/platform/shell + git snapshot (branch, dirty, recent commits), AGENTS.md resolution, project type detection) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/context/index.ts), [git-snapshot.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/context/git-snapshot.ts) | [ProjectRuleDetector.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProjectRuleDetector.swift) | Partial | AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md detection and an env section in the system prompt exist, but no git snapshot (branch/dirty/recent commits) is embedded in the env block. |
| Storage: SQLite session store (sessions, messages, todos, usage, input history, DWF journal, migration runner) | [sqlite-session-store.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/storage/session-store/sqlite-session-store.ts) | [ProfileDatabase.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProfileDatabase.swift), [ManagedAssetStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ManagedAssetStore.swift) | Full | SQLCipher profile database covers chats, messages, projects, memory, todos, attachments, artifacts, usage, embeddings, plus a managed encrypted attachment store; nuance: TurboSpark is profile-scoped and encrypted where Zcode is per-session plain sqlite. |
| Storage: workspace hook trust store (pid/start-time lockfile, trust records, atomic writes with rename retries) | [workspace-hook-trust-store.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/storage/workspace-hook-trust-store.ts) | [AppHookStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Hooks/AppHookStore.swift) | Full | Hook trust prompts and persisted trust entries exist for repo-sourced hooks; Zcode adds multi-process lockfile safety, a robustness-level difference. |
| Workflow run store (JSONL event/graph logs, run snapshots, index, definition files, artifacts/report writers) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/workflow/index.ts) | [WorkflowCronTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Automation/WorkflowCronTools.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark declares Workflow tool input types but the workflow runtime is not implemented and is filtered from advertising; implementing would involve a workflow engine plus run persistence like this store. |
| Session mailbox (file-based session-to-session envelopes with unread/read directories, strict session-id containment) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/mailbox/index.ts) | none | Missing | Subagents communicate in-process in TurboSpark; a mailbox would be a small filesystem or in-memory queue for cross-session messages. |
| Logging adapter (daily JSONL log files, 7-day retention cleanup, sensitive-key redaction, stderr sink) | [retention.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/logging/retention.ts), [serialize.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/logging/serialize.ts) | none | Missing | No unified file-log pipeline in the Swift app; a small log sink with redaction and retention would mainly aid debugging. |
| Provider adapter namespace (empty re-export stub) | [index.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/provider/index.ts) | none | Missing | Placeholder only; the provider registry lives in the @zcode/provider package (section 19). |

Scope remarks: this package is the CLI's external-I/O layer; its cloud-facing halves (provider/auth/model gateway/MCP OAuth/browser CDP) are legitimately absent from TurboSpark, which is local-FFI-only. The biggest concrete parity gaps worth closing are the streamable-HTTP MCP transport, PDF page rasterization and image budget compression for local vision models, and user/project .md custom command discovery.


## 6. Agent contracts, packages/contracts

Scope: `apps/zcode-cli/packages/contracts` - typed interface contracts (tool schemas, hook/plugin/
skill/permission/config/rewind/workflow/telemetry contracts). Parity judged at capability level.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |

Zcode's declaration is more formalized (budget/trace/cancellation policies); TurboSpark encodes the same knobs per executor. |
| File tool schemas (Read with offset/limit and image/PDF modes, Write, Edit str_replace, apply_patch, Glob, Grep) | [read.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/contracts/src/tools/read.ts), [edit.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/contracts/src/tools/edit.ts), [grep.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/contracts/src/tools/grep.ts) | [FileReadWriteTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/FileReadWriteTools.swift), [ApplyPatchExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/ApplyPatchExecutor.swift) | Full | Equivalent contracted tools; Zcode pins exact byte/line budgets in schema constants, TurboSpark enforces equivalents in executors plus FileSnapshotStore stale-write protection. |

Threading TraceContext through the agent loop would power the inspector with structured spans. |

The typed CoreError taxonomy could inspire structured error codes in TurboSpark's toast/error handling. |

Scope remarks: this package is almost entirely type/schema declarations, so parity was judged at the capability level each contract types. The largest genuine gaps are the dynamic workflow subsystem, custom slash prompt commands, cross-session messaging, stream recovery, and the off-peak cloud service; the strongest parity is in tool schemas, hooks, skills, plugins, MCP, permissions, cron, and compaction.


## 7. Dynamic workflow packages, packages/dynamic-workflow + dynamic-workflow-runtime

Scope: the workflow compiler, engine, sandbox harness, and their tool landing points.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Workflow facade API (embedded .d.ts surface the model writes scripts against: agent/ask, files/git/world.run, report, artifact, phase, args) | [dts.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/facade/dts.ts), [registry.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/facade/registry.ts) | [WorkflowCronTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Automation/WorkflowCronTools.swift) | Missing | Would involve defining a Swift-side script API surface plus an embedded type definition asset; TurboSpark's only analog is the declared Workflow tool struct (script/name/resumeFromRunId fields) with no engine behind it. |
| Script typechecking (virtual in-memory TS compiler host, script wrapped in async fn, facade libs embedded, no disk/process/fetch at compile time) | [compile.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/compiler/compile.ts) | none | Missing | Would require embedding a TypeScript-class checker in Swift (heavyweight); a narrower alternative is validating a restricted JSON/YAML workflow DSL instead of TS. |
| Authoring diagnostics (facade-siting, world.run literal-command, phase marker, duplicate actor, artifact serializability rules) | [facade-misuse.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/facade-misuse.ts), [world-run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/world-run.ts) | none | Missing | Depends on the compiler row; TurboSpark's Forge guardrails rescue malformed tool calls but perform no script static analysis. |
| Static analysis projections (taint fixpoint; site/causality/control-flow/handoff/actor graphs with mermaid emitters) | [analyze.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/analyze.ts), [causality-graph.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/causality-graph.ts) | none | Missing | Would need a script interpreter/IR first; the todo checklist and subagent cards show progress but no compiled plan graph. |
| Typed subagent results (ask<T> interface types synthesized to JSON Schema; validator with repair loop re-asks) | [synthesize.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/schema/synthesize.ts), [validate.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/schema/validate.ts) | [SubagentRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SubagentRunner.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark subagents return free text via Agent/Task tools; adding structured output would mean a schema registry and submit-result protocol in SubagentRunner. |
| Sandboxed script execution (child process + vm realm, NDJSON host bridge, no process/require/fetch, banned nondeterminism, frozen args) | [harness.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow-runtime/src/harness.ts), [protocol.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow-runtime/src/protocol.ts) | none | Missing | Would involve spawning a sandboxed interpreter process from Swift with a capability bridge; TurboSpark has no script execution substrate. |
| Deterministic run engine + replay (site id x ordinal identity, per-actor FIFO ordering, inputHash journal short-circuit, structured WorkflowError taxonomy) | [engine.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine.ts), [replay-order.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/replay-order.ts) | none | Missing | Requires the journal row first; TurboSpark's agent loop keeps in-memory turn state with epoch guards but nothing replayable. |
| Subagent orchestration primitives (named actors with persistent contexts, concurrent asks, Promise.all joins, fan-out loops and conditionals in script code) | [scheduler.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/scheduler.ts) | [AgentTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Tasks/AgentTools.swift), [BatchToolExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Tasks/BatchToolExecutor.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark runs concurrent background subagents (Batch tool caps at 25) but orchestration is one tool call or a flat batch: no loops, joins, data dependencies between results, or persistent named actor contexts. A script-driven layer over SubagentRunner would close the gap. |
| Durable run journal (run/actor/node/event records, SQLite impl, persisted token usage and caps) | [journal-memory.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/journal-memory.ts), [dwf-journal.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/storage/session-store/repositories/dwf-journal.ts) | [TaskManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Tasks/TaskManager.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark tracks background tasks and cron run history, but there is no per-run node/event journal supporting replay or audit; persisting subagent task graphs would be the first step. |
| Resume / crash recovery (byte-identical scriptHash check, replay of finished nodes, live re-dispatch, orphan-run reconciliation, ResumeWorkflowRun tool) | [engine.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine.ts), [resume-workflow-run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/resume-workflow-run.ts) | none | Missing | Would involve persisting per-step results and re-attaching sessions after process death; TurboSpark background agents die with the app. |
| Amendment of running workflows (AmendWorkflow supersedes a live run; import cache matches finished work; transcript seeding) | [amend-workflow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/amend-workflow.ts), [imported-cache.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/imported-cache.ts) | none | Missing | Would need journal replay plus actor-context lineage; closest TurboSpark analog is message edit/regenerate/branch of a single conversation, not multi-agent runs. |
| Escalation questions from subagents (parked-question registry, ResolveWorkflowQuestion tool; actor parks inside its ask awaiting the answer) | [workflow-escalation-registry.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/workflow-escalation-registry.ts), [resolve-workflow-question.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/resolve-workflow-question.ts) | [PlanningInteractiveTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Planning/PlanningInteractiveTools.swift), [InteractiveQuestionCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/InteractiveQuestionCardView.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark's AskUserQuestion cards work for the main agent in the transcript; background subagents have no channel to park on a blocking question and receive an answer. Routing subagent questions through the existing card UI would close it. |
| User confirmation gate before a run (clean compile precondition, approval prompt showing the phase graph and the pinned command set) | [create-workflow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/create-workflow.ts), [dynamic-workflow-gate.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/dynamic-workflow-gate.ts) | [AppToolPermissionEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Core/AppToolPermissionEngine.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark's permission engine asks per tool action; there is no whole-script approval with cost, stage graph and command set presented up front. A pre-run confirmation sheet reusing the permission prompt style is the natural fit. |
| phase() stage markers (compile-time literal names, phase-entered events, per-phase grouping on the run panel) | [phases.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/phases.ts) | none | Missing | Presentation-only grouping; would follow from implementing any workflow graph UI. |
| Mid-run reporting + artifact dashboards (journaled report() findings delivered across resume; artifact.chart/table/metrics/board presets) | [engine-report.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine-report.ts), [engine-artifacts.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine-artifacts.ts) | [ArtifactPanelView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ArtifactPanelView.swift), [SubagentLiveCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/SubagentLiveCardView.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark shows live subagent progress cards and previews tool-written HTML artifacts, but there is no journaled findings feed and no live chart/table/board dashboards built from reported items. |
| Artifact publication (artifact.file/markdown, versioned ids, bytes copied to a store, primary deliverable designation) | [engine-artifacts.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine-artifacts.ts), [workflow-artifact-publish.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/workflow-artifact-publish.ts) | [ArtifactWorktreeTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Projects/ArtifactWorktreeTools.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark's Artifact tool publishes a rich HTML artifact into a sandboxed WKWebView panel, but with no versioning, no multi-artifact sets per run, and no workspace-relative byte copying. |
| Journaled world reads (files.glob/read/grep and git.changedFiles/diff/status/log executed by the harness, recorded and replayed from the journal) | [engine-world.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/engine-world.ts), [workflow-git-world-read.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/workflow-git-world-read.ts) | [ChatGitSheets.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ChatGitSheets.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark has FileRead/Grep/Glob tools and git sheets (/diff, /log), but these are agent tool calls, not script observations journaled for deterministic replay; the gap is the journal integration. |
| world.run command execution (compile-time literal cmd pinned into the confirmed command set, fixed argv no shell, journaled per site+iteration) | [world-run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/analysis/world-run.ts), [protocol.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow-runtime/src/protocol.ts) | [ShellCommandRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/ShellCommandRunner.swift), [TerminalCommandClassifier.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/TerminalCommandClassifier.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark runs shell commands with a classifier and build-command allowlist, but nothing pins a per-run command set at approval time or journals executions for replay. |
| Live run control + adaptive concurrency (cancel/stop with resumable states, in-place max-concurrency retune, AIMD provider-cap governor) | [concurrency.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/dynamic-workflow/src/engine/concurrency.ts), [dynamic-workflow-run-retune.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/dynamic-workflow-run-retune.ts) | [AppModel+Cancellation.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+Cancellation.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark can stop background tasks and caps batch concurrency at 25, but concurrency is static and there is no rate-limit-driven throttling or live retune of a running job's parallelism. |
| Subagent model selection/pinning (per-actor model recorded and pinned across resume) | [workflow-actor-model.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/workflow-actor-model.ts) | [SubagentRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SubagentRunner.swift) | Partial | All TurboSpark subagents run on the single loaded local model; no per-run subagent model override exists, though an override would be straightforward since the runner already parameterizes sessions. |
| Saved workflows (SaveWorkflow/ListSavedWorkflows with project and global scope, frontmatter metadata, declared typed args validated before launch) | [save-workflow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/save-workflow.ts), [saved-workflows.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/zcode-protocol/saved-workflows.ts) | [SkillManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SkillManager.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark has no reusable parameterized workflow definitions; skills (SKILL.md with marketplace installs) are the closest reusable asset but carry no declared args or run semantics. Parameterized definitions could reuse the skills discovery/marketplace plumbing. |
| Run observation tools (GetWorkflowRun with roster/events/artifacts, ListWorkflowRuns, event pagination) | [get-workflow-run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/get-workflow-run.ts), [list-workflow-runs.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/list-workflow-runs.ts) | [TaskItemTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Tasks/TaskItemTools.swift) | Partial | TaskGet/TaskList/TaskOutput and the background agents strip expose task status and output, but there is no per-run node/event journal query, cost roster, or what-ran audit surface. |
| Runtime snippet evaluation (EvalWorkflowSnippet: synchronous transient compile+run of world reads and pure logic, confirmation only when commands are present) | [eval-workflow-snippet.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/eval-workflow-snippet.ts), [dynamic-workflow-snippet-service.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bootstrap/src/app/dynamic-workflow-snippet-service.ts) | [TerminalTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/TerminalTools.swift) | Partial | Closest is the Bash tool (and a REPL tool declared but not implemented); no sandboxed, journaled scratch evaluation of script logic against the real workspace exists. |
| Agent-facing workflow tool facade (CreateWorkflow/AmendWorkflow/SaveWorkflow/EvalWorkflowSnippet/GetWorkflowRun family with structured outputs) | [create-workflow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/create-workflow.ts), [amend-workflow.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/core/src/tool/handlers/amend-workflow.ts) | [WorkflowCronTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Automation/WorkflowCronTools.swift), [AutomationExecutors.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Automation/AutomationExecutors.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark declares a Workflow tool in its automation catalog but has no executor: dispatch never handles it and it is filtered from advertising; either implement the executor against a scheduler or remove the dead declaration. |

Scope remarks: this scope is a compiler-plus-engine library plus a sandbox harness whose user-facing landing points (tools, journal, escalation registry, saved-workflow store) live in sibling core/bootstrap/adapters packages, cited above. TurboSpark has no workflow engine at all: the Workflow tool is declared but unexecuted, so nearly everything here is Missing except where its generic subagent, permission, artifact, git, and shell surfaces overlap.


## 8. zcode-cli misc packages and tooling

Scope: node-repl-host, telemetry, debug, i18n, shared-types, browser-use-plugin, superpowers-plugin,
swift-bridge, bundled-skills, tools/, scripts/, dependencies/, repo meta files.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| node_repl MCP host (js tool: JS in a fresh Node worker kernel per call, timeout budgets, trace/workspace meta, subagent scoping) | [server.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/server.ts), [tool-contract.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/tool-contract.ts) | [TerminalTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/TerminalTools.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark declares a REPL tool schema but never implements it and filters it from advertising; parity would require a persistent JS runtime host (e.g. JavaScriptCore in-process) plus registration in the agent loop. |
| Browser Use bridge (broker protocol over a Unix socket exposing BrowserControl inside node_repl kernels; blocked in subagents) | [browser-bridge.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/browser-bridge.ts), [runtime-bridge.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/runtime-bridge.ts) | none | Missing | TurboSpark has no browser automation; its web surface is HTTP-only (WebFetch/HttpRequest). Implementing would involve a WKWebView or CDP driver plus a broker. |
| Computer Use (CUA) bridge + broker (OS-level app/window automation reachable from node_repl, token-authenticated local socket, per-call staleness guards) | [cua-bridge.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/cua-bridge.ts), [cua-broker.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/node-repl-host/src/cua-broker.ts) | none | Missing | No computer-use capability in TurboSpark. Would involve AXAccessibility APIs behind a broker, an SDK surface for the model, and raster-coordinate safety rules. |

| Model API recorder + privacy (call/attempt telemetry from model network events, sanitized errors, provider endpoint identity cache) | [model-api-recorder.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/telemetry/src/model-api-recorder.ts), [error-sanitizer.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/telemetry/src/error-sanitizer.ts) | [AppChatUsage.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppChatUsage.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark records usage outcomes per chat/day but has no per-request call/attempt recorder or error sanitization layer; extension point is the usage ledger plus server TTFB/TPS metrics. |

| Debug web UI (Vite + React trace waterfall/gantt rendering) | [dev.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/debug/scripts/dev.ts), [gantt-style.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/debug/src/gantt-style.ts) | none | Missing | Developer-only visualization; no equivalent in TurboSpark. |

Parity is inverse here: TurboSpark IS the native Swift app. |
| Bundled dynamic-workflows skill (SKILL.md plus patterns/examples forming the authoring contract for CreateWorkflow) | [SKILL.md](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/bundled-skills/skills/dynamic-workflows/SKILL.md) | [AppSkill.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppSkill.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark has a bundled skill scope and ships SKILL.md content, but has no CreateWorkflow/dynamic-workflow tooling for this skill to document; porting would first require a workflow compiler/runner. |

| Repo QA scripts (shadow replay of the real CLI sqlite db through cold recovery with conservation report, line-count lint gate) | [shadow-replay.mjs](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/scripts/shadow-replay.mjs), [count-ts-lines.mjs](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/scripts/count-ts-lines.mjs) | none | Missing | Internal delivery/test infrastructure; no counterpart, though TurboSpark could replay its SQLCipher DB through a migration checker similarly. |
| Bundled native search binaries (versioned bfs/ugrep/ripgrep archives with SHA256SUMS consumed by Glob/Grep tooling) | [SHA256SUMS](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/dependencies/native-search/SHA256SUMS), [dependencies/README.md](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/dependencies/README.md) | [FileSearchTools.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/File/FileSearchTools.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark implements code search natively in Swift (Syntext-indexed, ripgrep-format output) and ships no external binaries; Zcode gets battle-tested ugrep/ripgrep behavior cross-platform where TurboSpark maintains its own index. |
| Build orchestration and repo meta (turbo pipelines, pnpm workspace, SEA packaging entry points, skills-lock.json pinning dev skills, AGENTS.md, README) | [turbo.json](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/turbo.json), [skills-lock.json](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/skills-lock.json) | [SkillManager.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SkillManager.swift) | Missing | Build/CI plumbing with no user-facing parity; skills-lock.json pins dev-repo skills unrelated to end-user skill discovery, which TurboSpark covers via user/project/bundled/plugin scanning plus its git marketplace. |

Scope remarks: mostly infrastructure and developer tooling with little direct user-facing parity. The largest real gaps are the node_repl host with its Browser Use and Computer Use bridges (declared-only REPL in TurboSpark), the bundled dynamic-workflows skill, and the telemetry SDK (upload intentionally absent; local diagnostics are the partial counterpart). Localization favors TurboSpark (21 languages vs 2).


## 9. CLI and TUI, packages/cli + packages/tui

Scope: the terminal entrypoint and the OpenTUI-based terminal interface.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLI entrypoint and command router (help, version, doctor, login, logout, commands, plugins, skills, tui, server dispatch, exit watchdog) | [main.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/main.ts), [run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/run.ts) | none | Missing | TurboSpark is a GUI app with no terminal binary; a CLI surface would mean a Swift command-line target over the existing agent loop; per-command parity judged in rows below. |
| Interactive TUI app (OpenTUI React framework: startup screen, sidebar + transcript + composer shell, mouse and text selection) | [tui.tsx](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/tui/src/tui.tsx), [app-view.tsx](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/tui/src/app-view.tsx) | [ChatTranscriptView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ChatTranscriptView.swift), [PromptComposerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/PromptComposerView.swift) | Missing | The terminal TUI itself is Missing by design; the GUI delivers the same three-pane chat experience as Full equivalents (transcript, sidebar, composer). |
| Non-interactive one-shot prompt mode (-p/--prompt, --attach file/image/video/pdf paths, --cwd, default yolo mode, --target mapped to /goal) | [prompt-command.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/prompt-command.ts) | none | Missing | No headless turn runner in TurboSpark; nearest is the embedded OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible server that external clients can drive; a one-shot CLI runner plus JSON result would close it. |
| Output formats (text, --json summary, --output-format stream-json event stream) | [prompt-command.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/prompt-command.ts), [run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/run.ts) | [AppModel+LocalCommands.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+LocalCommands.swift) | Partial | /export json/html/markdown exports a finished chat, but there is no machine-readable per-run result line or live event stream; a JSON run-result endpoint on the embedded server would improve this. |
| Session resume/continue (--resume id, --continue latest session) | [resume.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/resume.ts) | [ProfileDatabase.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProfileDatabase.swift), [BuiltInSlashCommand.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/BuiltInSlashCommand.swift) | Full | Chats persist in SQLCipher and a /resume command exists; interactive GUI resume instead of an id-based CLI flag, same user capability. |
| Permission mode selection and tool rules (--mode build/plan/edit/yolo, /mode panel, --disallowedTools parsing) | [run.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/run.ts), [tui-command-state.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/tui-command-state.ts) | [AppToolPermissionEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Core/AppToolPermissionEngine.swift), [ToolCallClassifier.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Core/AgentMode/ToolCallClassifier.swift) | Full | /approval-mode plus the permission engine and local-model tool classifier cover mode and tool gating; vocabulary differs (ask/auto/agentAuto/permissive/fullAccess/readOnly vs build/plan/edit/yolo). |
| Locale and i18n (--locale, env auto-detection, /locale command) | [locale.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/locale.ts) | [AppLanguage.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/AppLanguage.swift) | Full | Both localize the UI; TurboSpark is broader with 21 languages plus RTL vs Zcode's en-US/zh-CN only. |
| Help, version, doctor | [help.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/help.ts) | [BuiltInSlashCommand.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/BuiltInSlashCommand.swift) | Partial | /help sheet and version info exist; no doctor-style runtime diagnostics; would mean a diagnostics sheet over engine/FFI/process state. |

An NSPasteboard image reader feeding the existing attachment pipeline is the concrete improvement. |
| Clipboard text copy (OSC52 escape + pbcopy/xclip fallbacks, selection copy) | [clipboard-text.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/src/clipboard-text.ts), [app-copy.ts](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/tui/src/app-copy.ts) | [MessageActionBarView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/MessageActionBarView.swift) | Full | Copy actions are pervasive via NSPasteboard; the TUI's OSC52 terminal fallback is a terminal-only mechanism with no GUI counterpart needed. |
| Subagent live view (read-only subagent transcript opened from sidebar strip, live progress) | [app-subagent-view.tsx](../../Zcode/apps/zcode-cli/packages/tui/src/app-subagent-view.tsx) | [SubagentLiveCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/SubagentLiveCardView.swift) | Full | Live subagent cards and transcript inspection exist; the TUI's read-only pane swap is a terminal-specific presentation. |

Scope remarks: cli is the terminal entrypoint (args, headless prompt mode, plugins/skills/commands/hooks CLIs, process plumbing) and tui is an OpenTUI React terminal app; both map onto existing TurboSpark GUI capabilities for chat, themes, composer, and sidebar, while headless modes, output formats, browser automation, and dynamic workflows have no TurboSpark equivalent. Verified TurboSpark specifics: 37 built-in slash commands, NSPasteboard never reads image data (clipboard image paste Partial), and Workflow tools are declared but unimplemented.

cua-permission.

Scope remarks: settings is the largest ui folder (215 files) and maps section-for-section onto TurboSpark's 16 tabs, with cloud-only sections (model provider accounts, browser/CUA, coding-plan usage) as the honest gaps. TurboSpark has no command palette/quickpick (verified by grep), so the Cmd+K surface is the weakest shell parity area. Onboarding, login, remote connection, feedback, bots and CUA permission flows are cloud/remote features with no TurboSpark equivalent by design.

Scope: `packages/ui/src` chat-input-toolbar, prompt-editor, mentions, ToolCallBlocks, presentation,
components, hooks, lib, i18n, assets.

Scope remarks: chat-input-toolbar is the one area with genuinely cloud-only features (coding-plan quotas, entitlements) that are Missing by design. The largest transferable gaps are composer drag-and-drop, the 7-category mention panel with IME handling, mermaid rendering, and CUA/workflow tool cards. TurboSpark leads on locales (21 vs 2), voice dictation (../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/PromptComposerControls.swift), and TTS read-aloud, which Zcode does not have in this scope.

Scope remarks: the git review/diff/commit-list/timeline half has real TurboSpark counterparts (worktree inspector + git sheets) with the main gaps being staging-aware views, any commit/push workflow, and lane-based graphing. The entire browser-use folder has no TurboSpark equivalent. Zcode's own GitPane stage/unstage/discard actions are placeholder stubs and its last-turn dataset is an unwired skeleton, so neither side offers staging manipulation today.

Real local analogs exist only for credential storage (Keychain vs encrypted credentials.json), model selection/settings CRUD (single local engine vs provider registry), client scenes (static prompt examples), and legacy store migration.

| Onboarding records (per-user onboarding quiz persistence, settings backfill of occupation/preferences) | packages/services/src/onboarding/onboardingRecordService.ts; packages/services/src/onboarding/onboardingRecord.ts | none | Missing | A small JSON record plus a first-launch flow; TurboSpark has no onboarding or accounts. |
| Cloud API client (HTTP client for backend endpoints: origin resolution, JWT handling, request-id headers, error classification) | packages/services/src/providers/api/nodeApiClient.ts; packages/services/src/providers/api/apiEndpoints.ts | none | Missing | Inherently cloud-service infrastructure; TurboSpark's only HTTP surface is the embedded local server. |
| Remote sync preflight (verify write access to remote sync directories via marker files) | packages/services/src/remote-sync/remoteSyncWriteAccess.ts | none | Missing | Only meaningful for remote/multi-machine workspaces; the local subset would be a permission pre-check before bulk writes. |
| Runtime environment tools (login-shell env capture, PATH-based runtime tool resolution, proxy env, self-signed CA for agent subprocess) | packages/services/src/runtime-tools/runtimeLoginShellEnvCapture.ts; packages/services/src/runtime-tools/runtimeToolResolver.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/ShellOutputFormatting.swift | Partial | TurboSpark Bash inherits the parent process PATH/env, so a GUI-launched app can miss homebrew tools. Capturing the user's login shell env at startup and prepending standard paths would improve it. |
| System service (system info, integrated-terminal shell enumeration, intranet probe, SSH config alias resolution) | packages/services/src/system/systemService.ts; packages/services/src/system/sshConfigAlias.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Chrome/StatusBarView.swift | Partial | TurboSpark surfaces RAM/CPU/thermal status but has no integrated terminal (only background shells) and no SSH targets; a shell-picker for background shells would be the local subset. |
| Window host controller (task list actions pin/archive/delete/mark-read/open/resume for a tasks window) | packages/services/src/window-controller/windowController.ts | none | Missing | TurboSpark is a single-window app; multi-window task management would use openWindow + shared AppModel state. |
| Data path rules (centralized data base dir resolution with env override, .zcode root, conversation workspace dir) | packages/services/src/paths.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppStorageRoot.swift | Full | Both centralize the one on-disk root every store lives under; Zcode adds runtime relocation. |
| Service registry and RPC plumbing (typed service descriptors, service collection, host wiring, memory diagnostics) | packages/services/src/descriptors.ts; packages/services/src/collection.ts | none | Missing | Build/process infrastructure with no user-facing parity; TurboSpark calls AppModel directly with no RPC layer. |
| Shared type declarations (ambient module typings for bundled npm deps) | packages/services/src/types/yazl.d.ts | none | Missing | Pure TypeScript compile plumbing; no runtime feature. |

Scope remarks: mostly service-layer wiring over the Zcode agent runtime plus several cloud-only features (bots, providers, feedback, onboarding, conversation share/telemetry, off-peak) that TurboSpark intentionally lacks as a local-only app. Strong local parity exists for the agent runtime/session stack, skills, subagents, automation, and settings.

Scope remarks: src/main is mostly flat infrastructure; the only subfolders are browserView/ (embedded browser) and mcpUserDirectory/, plus src/scheduler/ (cron utility process). Roughly half the scope (telemetry, deep links, embedded browser, Chrome import, CUA permissions, remote sessions) has no TurboSpark analog because those are Electron/cloud-era capabilities; conversely Electron must solve protocol handlers, tray, and multi-window work that SwiftUI gets from AppKit for free.

Scope remarks: mostly Electron plumbing (preload bridge, window host process, telemetry, remote workspace transport) with no TurboSpark equivalent because TurboSpark calls AppModel directly in one process. Genuine user-facing parity exists for native file dialogs, OS reveal/open integrations, auto-update, and desktop commands; partial parity for startup/migration UX, notifications, permissions onboarding, and storage management.


## 18. Protocol types, packages/shared

Scope: `packages/shared/src` - shared protocol/type definitions (zcode-protocol, zcode-protocol-v4,
browser-use, node). Judged at the underlying capability level; TurboSpark has no wire protocol
(monolithic app) but implements most underlying capabilities.

| Feature | Zcode source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Core RPC envelope and handshake (typed request/notification/response/error frames with trace headers) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts; packages/shared/src/handshake.ts | none | Missing | Monolithic app needs no internal wire protocol; the closest wire surface is the embedded OpenAI/Anthropic/Ollama-compatible HTTP server (../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Server/ServerEndpointCatalog.swift). |
| Session lifecycle RPC (create/resume/list/send/stop/close/setModel/setMode; immediate vs deferred persistence) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+Generation.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ProfileDatabase.swift | Partial | Underlying capabilities (create/resume/persist/multi-turn, SQLCipher-backed chat store) are Full; the wire contract is not, since agent and UI are one process. |
| Live session event stream (25 typed events in seq-numbered envelopes with subscribe, afterSeq replay, included snapshot) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+Generation.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/StreamingStatusFooterView.swift | Partial | Streaming text/tool progress/steer queue are rendered live, but there is no persisted seq-replayable event log; resume rebuilds from the database. Journaling turn events with sequence numbers would enable replay. |
| Interactive permission and user-input handshake (permission.requested/resolved with risk levels; userInput text/choice/confirm) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Core/AppToolPermissionEngine.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/InteractiveQuestionCardView.swift | Full | Same capability end to end: risk-classified permission cards with options and session always-allow, plus AskUserQuestion cards; in-process instead of RPC. |
| Compaction protocol (session/compact, compactBoundary messages, history targets) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppChatCompaction.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AgentEfficiency.swift | Full | Auto and manual /compact with boundary bookkeeping and keep-recent clamp. |
| Goal protocol (goal actions, goal stats with tokens/tool calls/iterations, verification timeline) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+GoalLoop.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ChatGoal.swift | Full | /goal state machine with evaluator judge loop, verdicts, and token accounting. |
| Subagent protocol (running/ended states, child session attribution, batch results) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SubagentRunner.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Tasks/TaskManager.swift | Full | Foreground/background concurrent subagents with live cards and ended-state history. |
| Background task protocol (background bash jobs, control items, elapsed/status merge, cancel) | packages/shared/src/background-bash-jobs.ts; packages/shared/src/background-task-controls.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/BackgroundShellManager.swift; ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Terminal/TerminalTools.swift | Full | Background shells with BashOutput/KillShell, a background shells strip, and cancellation. |
| Message edit/fork/rewind protocol (session/fork, editUserQuery, checkpoint.created, stable fork targets) | packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol/index.ts; packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol-v4/fork.ts | ../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+MessageEditing.swift | Full | Edit, regenerate, branch/fork, variants cover the capability; no cross-process fork-target validation schema, which is fine in-process. |
| Browser/computer-use automation protocol (45+ command methods, iab/extension/cdp backends, element-ref DOM snapshots, CUA permissions) | packages/shared/src/browser-use/commands.ts; packages/shared/src/browser-use/backend.ts; packages/shared/src/zcode-protocol-v4/cuaPermission.ts | none | Missing | No browser or computer-use agent in TurboSpark.
